It had no awareness of file changes on disk from external operations like `git checkout`.\n\n**Fix**: Added a `FileSystemWatcher` in the `listen()` method that watches for `**/*.{ts,tsx,js,jsx}` file changes:\n- `onDidChange`: When a file changes on disk and has a synced buffer, re-opens the buffer (sends new content to TS server) and requests fresh diagnostics\n- `onDidCreate`: Same as change - handles newly created files\n- `onDidDelete`: Closes the resource and clears its diagnostics when a file is deleted\n\n### 2. `src/vs/workbench/api/browser/mainThreadDiagnostics.ts` (Core Platform)\n\n**Problem**: The main thread diagnostics bridge had no mechanism to clear stale markers when files changed on disk. Even if extensions would eventually provide fresh diagnostics, old markers would persist until explicitly cleared.\n\n**Fix**: Added an `IFileService` dependency and subscribed to `onDidFilesChange`. When files are updated or deleted on disk, the handler iterates through all active diagnostic owners and clears markers for affected files. This ensures the Problems panel immediately reflects the current state while waiting for extensions to provide fresh diagnostics.\n\n### 3. `src/vs/workbench/api/test/browser/mainThreadDiagnostics.test.ts` (Tests)\n\nUpdated all 3 test instantiations of `MainThreadDiagnostics` to provide the new `IFileService` mock parameter with `onDidFilesChange = Event.None` (a no-op event that never fires, appropriate for existing tests that don't test file change behavior).\n\n### Verification\n\n- Full project type-checks cleanly with `tsc --noEmit` (no compilation errors)\n- All 3 files modified; 56 lines added, 0 removed\n- No changes to any other files or test expectations\n- Test mocks properly provide the new dependency"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":268,"cache_read_input_tokens":135389,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":268},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1163e45c-6e3e-4223-90f8-d10bfa3df86f","uuid":"c8011ff9-9a26-479b-8a41-ca75fb2e03b3"}
